<div class="linked-index-list primary-gradient">
<h2 id="_linkListHeader">
        Products
        <a cookie-tracking="ref_page_type=HP;ref_page_sub_type=HP;ref_page_id=%7BD998A0F9-B086-43EE-8377-071A844DD0FC%7D;ref_page_event=View%20All%20Products" href="/products/en">View All</a>

<h2 class="title">
<a href="https://www.digikey.in/en/resources/technology/powerful-gan-solution">Power GaN Solution</a>
</h2>
<p><a href="/en/supplier-centers/e/epc" target="_self">EPC</a>’s GaN FETs can achieve higher switching speeds in a smaller package when compared to silicon enabling more efficient and power dense designs. In order to take advantage of these GaN characteristics, <a href="/en/supplier-centers/t/texas-instruments" target="_self">Texas Instrument</a>s offers the LMG1210 FET driver. This is a 50 MHz half-bridge driver optimized for high frequency GaN circuits. When looking for inductors to pair with it, <a href="/en/supplier-centers/w/wurth-electronics" target="_self">Würth Elektronik</a>’s WE-MAPI and WE-MAIA series are a great choice with their high current capacity and compact size.</p>
